## No. 1 Fitzjohn Square / 菲茨约翰广场一号

===Original===
A few moments later and Ravenspur’s brougham was being rapidly driven in the direction of the Grand Hotel. No words were spoken on the journey, but Ravenspur did not fail to notice how his companion shook and quivered as the shouts of the newsboys reached her ears. It seemed as if all London had given itself over to this last sensational tragedy. It was as if thousands of strange rough hands were pressing upon the still bleeding wound. To an intensely sympathetic nature like Ravenspur’s, the relief of the destination was great. At his suggestion of food his companion shuddered. The mere idea of it turned her physically sick. Utterly worn out and exhausted she dropped into a chair. There was a light now of something like madness in her eyes. The doctor bustled in presently with something in his hand. Mrs. Delahay drank the medicine in a mechanical way, scarcely knowing what she was doing. Then, gradually, her rigid limbs relaxed, and the staring dark eyes were closed.

===Chinese===
过了不多一会儿，拉文斯珀（Ravenspur）那辆四轮轿式马车便已朝着格兰德饭店（Grand Hotel）的方向疾驰而去。一路上两人一言不发，可当报童们的叫卖声传进她耳中时，同伴如何浑身发抖、阵阵战栗，拉文斯珀全看在眼里。仿佛整个伦敦都已一头栽进了这桩最新的轰动惨案之中；又仿佛有成千上万只陌生而粗暴的手，正按在那还在渗血的伤口上。对拉文斯珀这样一副极易感同身受的心肠来说，抵达目的地实在是一大解脱。他提议叫点吃的，同伴却打了个寒噤——光是这一个念头，就让她生理上直欲作呕。她精疲力竭、心力交瘁，一屁股跌坐进椅子里。这时她眼中闪着一种近乎疯狂的光。不多时，医生手里拿着什么东西，脚步匆匆地进来了。德拉海夫人（Mrs. Delahay）机械地把药喝下，几乎不知道自己在做什么。随后，渐渐地，她僵直的四肢松弛下来，那双圆睁的黑眼睛闭上了。

===Original===
“She’ll do now for some time,” the doctor whispered. “I have telephoned for a nurse who may be here now at any moment. Don’t let me detain you. I have got my motor outside, and in any case I must remain till the nurse arrives.”

===Chinese===
"她这会儿可以支撑一阵子了，"医生低声说，"我已经打电话请了一位护士，随时都可能到。别让我耽搁了您。我的汽车就在外面，反正护士到之前，我必须留在这儿。"

===Original===
“That is very good of you,” Ravenspur murmured. “As far as I am concerned I should like to make some inquiries. I have known Delahay now for the last five years; indeed, it was I who persuaded him to take up his quarters in London. It seems a terrible thing that so promising a career should be cut short like this. That man would have come to the top of his profession, and, so far as I know, he hadn’t a single enemy in the world. Perhaps, by this time, the Scotland Yard people may have found a clue.”

===Chinese===
"您真是太好了，"拉文斯珀喃喃道，"至于我，我想去查探一番。我认识德拉海（Delahay）至今已有五年了；说实话，当初正是我劝他把住处安在伦敦的。这样一桩大有可为的事业竟就此戛然中断，实在是可怕。他那个人本可以在他那一行里爬到顶点，而且据我所知，他在这个世上连一个仇人都没有。也许到这个时候，苏格兰场（Scotland Yard）的人已经找到线索了。"

===Original===
Ravenspur drove straight away to Fitzjohn Square, and made his way through the crowd of morbid folks who had gathered outside. As he expected, he found the house in the hands of the police. Inspector Dallas came forward and greeted him respectfully.

===Chinese===
拉文斯珀径直驱车赶往菲茨约翰广场（Fitzjohn Square），从聚在屋外那群怀着病态好奇心的闲人中间挤了过去。果然不出他所料，那座房子已落入警方手中。达拉斯探长（Inspector Dallas）迎上前来，毕恭毕敬地向他问候。

===Original===
“This is a terrible affair, my lord,” he said.

===Chinese===
"这是桩可怕的案子，大人。"他说。

===Original===
“Ghastly,” Ravenspur exclaimed. “It was a great shock when Mrs. Delahay came round to me this morning. And the strange part of the whole business is that I was in this very house myself, quite alone, till half-past one. Perhaps I had better explain the circumstances to you, as the knowledge might prove useful.﻿ ﻿… And now you know all about it. Mind you, I saw nothing; I did not hear a sound. Indeed, I am quite convinced that there was no one on the premises when I left.”

===Chinese===
"骇人听闻，"拉文斯珀感叹道，"今天早晨德拉海夫人跑到我那里去的时候，我真是大吃一惊。而整件事里最蹊跷的是，我自己昨晚就在这幢房子里，孤零零一个人，一直待到一点半。或许我还是把当时的情况对您说明一遍为好，这些情况也许会派上用场。……好了，现在您全都知道了。请您注意，我什么也没有看见；我一点声息也没有听见。的确，我深信不疑：我离开的时候，宅子里一个人也没有。"

===Original===
“But you had no means of making sure,” the inspector protested. “The miscreants might have been here all the time. They might have been hiding in a room upstairs waiting for you to go.”

===Chinese===
"可您当时没法子确认哪，"探长反驳道，"那些歹徒兴许从头到尾都待在这儿。他们可能一直躲在楼上的一个房间里，专等您离开。"

===Original===
“They might have attacked me as far as that goes,” Ravenspur replied. “My word, the mere suggestion of it turns one cold.”

===Chinese===
"照这么一说，他们连我也可能下手啊，"拉文斯珀答道，"我的天，光是这么一想，就叫人遍体生寒。"

===Original===
“At any rate, they were not after your lordship,” the inspector said, thoughtfully. “Of course, I am assuming for the sake of argument that the murderer, or murderers, were actually here when you arrived last night. If so, the whole thing was carefully premeditated. These people had no quarrel with you, and, therefore, they did not molest you. All the same, they wanted to get rid of you, or they would not have cut off the light.”

===Chinese===
"不管怎么说，他们图的可不是大人您，"探长若有所思地说，"当然，我这样假设只是为了便于推论：昨晚您到达的时候，凶手——不管是一个还是几个——确实就在这幢房子里。倘若如此，整件事情就是经过精心预谋的。这些人同您无冤无仇，因此没有惊动您。尽管如此，他们还是想打发您离开，否则就不会把电灯弄灭了。"

===Original===
“But did they cut off the light?” Ravenspur asked.

===Chinese===
"可那电灯当真是他们弄灭的吗？"拉文斯珀问道。

===Original===
“That we can prove in a moment. I am going on the theory that these people wanted to get you out of the way, so they short-circuited the current and left you in darkness. That was a very useful expedient, and had the desired effect. I am very glad you told me this because it may be the means of putting us on the track of important evidence. But let us go down to the basement, and examine the electric meter.”

===Chinese===
"这一点我们马上就能证实。我目前依据的推断是：这些人想让您走开，于是把电流弄成短路，把您撂在黑暗里。这一手着实管用，也收到了预期的效果。您把这件事告诉我，我很高兴，因为这或许能使我们循迹追查到重要的证据。走，我们下地下室去，检查一下电表。"

===Original===
Ravenspur followed his companion down the dark steps leading to the basement, and Inspector Dallas struck a light. Then, with a grim smile, he pointed to a cable which led from the meter to the different rooms on the upper floors. The cable had been clean cut with some sharp instrument, a fracture which must have been recently made, for the main wire to the cable gleamed like gold.

===Chinese===
拉文斯珀跟着他的同伴走下通往地下室的昏暗台阶，达拉斯探长擦亮了一根火柴。随后，他带着一丝冷笑，指了指一根从电表通向楼上各个房间的电缆。电缆被某种锋利的工具齐齐切断，断口必定是新近才造成的，因为通往电缆的主线在断处泛着金子般的光。

===Original===
“So far, so good,” Dallas said. “We have proved by yonder demonstration that these people were here last night whilst you were actually at work in the studio.”

===Chinese===
"到目前为止，一切都说得通，"达拉斯说，"方才那一验已经证明：昨晚您还在画室里工作的时候，这些人就已经在这幢房子里了。"

===Original===
“That puzzles me more than ever,” Ravenspur replied. “Why did they not get rid of me an hour before, which they could have done equally as well, by the same simple expedient?”

===Chinese===
"这倒叫我比先前更加摸不着头脑了，"拉文斯珀答道，"他们为什么不在一个钟头之前就打发我走呢？用同样简便的法子，他们照样办得到啊。"

===Original===
“Simply because they could afford to wait till half-past one. You may depend upon it that Mr. Delahay’s movements were absolutely known to them. They were perfectly well aware of the fact that he was not expected here till some time past half-past one. It is not a nice insinuation to make, but when Mr. Delahay left his hotel at midnight, he had not the slightest intention of coming straight here. Doubtless he had important business which was likely to last him an hour and a half, and for some reason or other he did not want his wife to know what it was. Speaking as one man of the world to another, Mr. Delahay’s excuse for getting out strikes me as being rather a shallow one. Surely a married man, more or less on his honeymoon, does not want to visit an empty house after midnight. Surely he could have waited till daylight.”

===Chinese===
"只因为他们等得起，尽可以等到一点半。您尽管相信，德拉海先生的一举一动，他们全都了如指掌。他们十分清楚，一点半过后好一阵子，他都不会到这儿来。这话听起来有些诛心，可德拉海先生午夜离开旅馆的时候，压根儿就没打算直接回这里来。毫无疑问，他有一桩要紧事要办，多半得耗上一个半钟头，而出于这样那样的缘故，他不想让妻子知道那是什么事。作为一个见过世面的人对另一个见过世面的人，说句直话——德拉海先生那个出门的借口，在我看来未免有些站不住脚。一个成了家的男人，怎么说还在蜜月里，总不至于半夜三更跑来看一幢空房子吧。他总归可以等到天亮的。"

===Original===
“Then you think he went out to keep an appointment?”

===Chinese===
"那么您是认为，他出门是去赴约了？"

===Original===
“I feel quite convinced of it, your lordship. And, moreover, the appointment was a secret one of which Mrs. Delahay was to know nothing. I will go still further, and say that Mr. Delahay came here after you had gone this morning to keep an appointment. It is just possible that he might have been in the house during your presence here. It is just possible that he cut the cable himself.”

===Chinese===
"这一点我深信不疑，大人。不但如此，那还是一场秘密约会，德拉海夫人本来丝毫不该知道。我还要更进一步说：德拉海先生今天早晨就是在您走了之后，来这里赴约的。就在您待在这儿的当口，他完全可能已经在这幢房子里了。那根电缆，也完全有可能是他自己割断的。"

===Original===
“Ah, but that won’t quite do,” Ravenspur protested. “When I came out of the house this morning I saw that the front door was carefully fastened, and I am prepared to swear that the latchkey which Mrs. Delahay found this morning was not in the lock then. No, no; I am quite sure that poor Delahay must have come here after I left. I am not prepared to contest your theory that my unfortunate friend came here to keep an appointment. Indeed, the presence of the latchkey in the door proves that he was in a hurry, and perhaps a little upset, or he would not have committed the mistake of leaving the key behind him. But after all, said and done, this is merely conjecture on our part. Have you found anything yourself that is likely to give you a clue?”

===Chinese===
"啊，这可说不通，"拉文斯珀提出异议，"今天早晨我从房子里出来时，亲眼看见前门关得严严实实；而且我可以起誓，德拉海夫人今早发现的那把弹簧锁钥匙，当时并不在锁眼里。不，不；我敢肯定，可怜的德拉海一定是在我走了之后才来的。您推测我这位不幸的朋友是来赴约，我不打算争辩。的确，门上插着钥匙这一点，恰恰证明他来去匆匆，也许还有点心慌意乱，否则就不会犯下把钥匙落在身后的错误了。可归根结底，这些都不过是我们俩的臆测。您自己可曾发现什么，有可能给你们提供线索的东西？"

===Original===
Inspector Dallas hesitated just for a moment.

===Chinese===
达拉斯探长迟疑了片刻。

===Original===
“Perhaps I ought not to mention it,” he said, “but I am sure I can rely upon your lordship’s discretion. When I was called this morning I found Mr. Delahay lying on the floor of the studio quite dead. So far as we could see there were no marks of violence on the body except a small puncture over the heart, which appears to have been made with some very fine instrument. But, of course, we can’t speak definitely on that point till we have had the inquest. As far as we can judge, something like a struggle must have taken place, because the loose carpets on the floor were in great disorder, and one or two articles of furniture had been overturned. You may say that this proves nothing, except that violence was used. But in the hand of the dead man we found something that might be useful to us. Perhaps you would like to see it.”

===Chinese===
"这个我或许本不该说的，"他说道，"不过我确信大人您口风紧，靠得住。今早我被叫来的时候，发现德拉海先生已经死透了，直挺挺躺在画室的地板上。就我们所能看到的，尸体上没有任何暴力痕迹，只有心口上方一个小小的孔，看上去是用某种极细的利器刺成的。当然，验尸之前，这一点我们还无法断言。依我们判断，当时想必发生过一番类似搏斗的动静，因为地板上平铺的地毯乱成一团，还有一两件家具翻倒了。您尽可以说，这什么也证明不了，只能说明动用过暴力。可是在死者的手里，我们发现了也许对我们有用的东西。您或许愿意看一看。"

===Original===
Lord Ravenspur intimated that he should. From a pocketbook Dallas produced a photograph, carte de visite size, which had been torn into half a dozen pieces. The photograph was considerably faded, and in the tearing the actual face itself had been ripped out of all recognition. Still, judging from the small fragments, it was possible to make out that the picture had been that of a woman. One scrap of card bore the words “and Co., Melbourne.” The rest of the lettering had apparently vanished.

===Chinese===
拉文斯珀勋爵表示愿意。达拉斯从随身记事本里取出一张照片，名片大小，已被撕成了五六片。照片褪色得厉害，撕扯之下，人脸本身已被抠挖得无从辨认。不过，从那些细小的碎片仍能看出，照片上拍的曾是一个女人。其中一小片卡纸上留着"and Co., Melbourne"（"……公司，墨尔本"）的字样，其余的字样显然已经不知去向。

===Original===
“This must have been taken a long time ago,” Ravenspur said. “It is so terribly faded.”

===Chinese===
"这张照片一定是很久以前拍的了，"拉文斯珀说，"褪色褪得这样厉害。"

===Original===
“Not necessarily, my lord,” Dallas said. “We know very little about that photograph as yet except that it was taken in Australia. Of course, it is fair to assume that the picture is an old one judging from the colouring, but your lordship must not forget that foreign photographs are always much fainter than those taken in this country, because the light is so much stronger and more brilliant. At any rate, the fact remains that we found those fragments tightly clenched in Mr. Delahay’s left hand, all of which points to some intrigue, with a woman at the bottom of it. Of course, I know nothing whatever about Mr. Delahay’s moral character﻿—”

===Chinese===
"那倒未必，大人，"达拉斯说，"那张照片的情况，我们眼下还知道得很少，只知道它是在澳大利亚（Australia）拍的。当然，从色调来看，推断这是一张旧照片不无道理；但大人您不可忘记，国外拍的照片总比在我们国内拍的浅淡得多，因为那边的光线要强烈明亮得多。无论如何，事实仍旧是：我们发现那些碎片紧紧攥在德拉海先生的左手里，而这一切都指向某桩私情纠葛——根子上是个女人。当然，关于德拉海先生的人品操行，我是一无所知——"

===Original===
“Then I’ll tell you,” Ravenspur said sharply. “My late friend was the soul of honour. He was a very quick, passionate man, and he inherited his temper from his Italian mother. But the man was incapable of anything mean or dishonourable. He was genuinely in love with his wife, and cared nothing for any other woman. How that photograph came into his possession I don’t know. Probably we never shall know. But you can at once dismiss from your mind the suspicion that Delahay was mixed up in that vulgar kind of business. Now, is there anything more you can tell me?”

===Chinese===
"那就让我来告诉您，"拉文斯珀厉声说道，"我这位亡友是信义的化身。他是个性子很急、火气很大的人，那脾气是从他意大利裔的母亲那里继承来的。可这个人决做不出任何卑鄙或不名誉的事。他真心实意地爱着自己的妻子，对任何别的女人都不屑一顾。那张照片怎么到他手里的，我不知道；恐怕我们永远不会知道。但您可以立刻把这层疑心从脑子里打发掉——怀疑德拉海卷进了那类下流的勾当。好了，您还有什么别的可以告诉我吗？"

===Original===
“Well, no,” Dallas said, after a short pause. “There is nothing that strikes me, no suggestions that seem to need a doctor’s opinion. We shall find that the cause of death is the small puncture over the heart that I spoke of. To hazard an opinion, it might be caused by one of those glass stilettos﻿—the Corsican type of weapon where the blade is snapped off in the wound. It leaves the smallest mark, and no blood follows﻿—a difficult thing to trace without great care. Of course, the postmortem﻿—”

===Chinese===
"嗯，没有了，"达拉斯略停片刻，说道，"没有什么能引起我注意的东西，也没有什么疑点看起来需要医生的意见。我们会查明，死因就是我方才说的心口上那个细小的孔。斗胆揣测一句，那可能是那种玻璃匕首干的——科西嘉（Corsican）式的凶器，刃子一刺进伤口便折断在里面。它留下的痕迹极小，又不淌血——不格外细心，是很难追查出来的。当然，验尸结果——"

## XXIV A Blood Relation / 第二十四章 血亲

===Original===
Walter choked down an ugly word that rose to his lips. He resented the intrusion just at a moment when he particularly desired to be alone with Vera. Who was it, he wondered, who came so late? And who rang so imperiously and persistently for admission? He flung back bolt and chain, and opened the door. With her nerves all unstrung, and with a certain intuition of impending calamity upon her, Vera had followed him into the hall. She had dried her eyes now; she showed little sign of her recent agitation. She heard Walter’s exclamation as he recognised the intruder.

===Chinese===
沃尔特（Walter）把一句涌到嘴边的难听话硬生生咽了下去。偏偏在他一心想同薇拉（Vera）单独相处的当口有人上门搅扰，他不由得心头火起。他暗自纳闷，这么晚了，来者会是谁？又是谁把门铃按得这般盛气凌人、这般没完没了，非要进来不可？他拉开插销和门链，把门打开。薇拉早已心神涣散，心头又隐隐预感大祸将至，便跟着他来到门厅。这会儿她已拭干眼泪，方才那阵激动的痕迹几乎看不出来了。她听见沃尔特认出来客时发出的一声惊呼。

===Original===
“Good heavens, it is Mrs. Delahay!” he cried. “What can you want here at this hour?”

===Chinese===
“天哪，是德拉海夫人（Mrs. Delahay）！”他叫道，“这么晚了，您来这儿有什么事？”

===Original===
“The studio,” Mrs. Delahay gasped. “Get to the studio at once. If your uncle should happen to be there﻿—”

===Chinese===
“画室，”德拉海夫人喘着气说，“快去画室。万一您叔父碰巧在里头——”

===Original===
“You can reassure yourself on that point,” Walter said. “Lord Ravenspur is at present in his bedroom.”

===Chinese===
“这一点您尽管放心，”沃尔特说，“拉文斯珀勋爵（Lord Ravenspur）此刻正在他卧室里呢。”

===Original===
Maria Delahay pressed her hand to her heart. She gave a little gasp of relief. She was too breathless to explain. All she needed now was a chair to support her failing limbs. As Walter stood there it flashed upon him that something wrong must be taking place in connection with the studio. He had not forgotten the vivid incident of the other night. Perhaps at this very moment the clue to the puzzle was in his hands. He turned round, and his gaze fell upon Vera, who was watching Mrs. Delahay curiously.

===Chinese===
玛丽亚·德拉海（Maria Delahay）一手按住心口，轻吁一声，如释重负。她喘得透不过气来，一时无法解释。此刻她只求有一把椅子，好撑住那双发软的腿。沃尔特站在那里，脑中忽然闪过一个念头：画室那边一定是出了乱子。前几夜那桩惊心动魄的变故，他还没有忘记。也许此时此刻，这个谜团的线索就攥在他手里。他转过身来，目光落在薇拉身上——她正满腹狐疑地打量着德拉海夫人。

===Original===
“Take this lady into the drawing-room,” he said, “and wait till I come back. I shan’t be very long.”

===Chinese===
“把这位夫人扶到客厅去，”他说，“等我回来。我去去就来。”

===Original===
Vera came forward with a sympathetic smile upon her face. A light was shining on her features. Maria Delahay could see how fair and sweet she was. And so this, she thought, was her sister’s child. This was the girl from whom her mother had voluntarily separated herself for upwards of eighteen years. It seemed impossible, incredible to believe, but there it was. And the girl’s hand was under Mrs. Delahay’s arm now. She was being gently assisted as far as the drawing-room.

===Chinese===
薇拉迎上前来，脸上带着怜惜的微笑，神情间焕发着光彩。玛丽亚·德拉海看得出，这姑娘是多么标致、多么可人。她心里想：原来这就是她姐姐的孩子；这就是那位做母亲的自愿舍弃、一别十八年有余的姑娘。这桩事似乎全然不可能、全然难以置信，可事实就摆在眼前。此刻，姑娘的手正搀在德拉海夫人的臂弯里，一路轻轻扶着她，向客厅走去。

===Original===
“I am sure you are Mrs. Delahay,” Vera said, in her most sympathetic voice. “If all had gone well we should have met before now. I cannot tell you how sorry I am for you. I do hope this dreadful mystery will be cleared up before long. And now can I get you anything? I suppose you came to see Lord Ravenspur?”

===Chinese===
“您准是德拉海夫人吧，”薇拉用她最体贴的声调说，“倘若诸事顺遂，我们本该早就见过面了。您遭了这样的不幸，我心里说不出的难过。我衷心盼望这桩可怕的疑案不久便能水落石出。您眼下需要些什么吗？我猜，您是来见拉文斯珀勋爵的吧？”

===Original===
Maria Delahay hesitated for a moment. There was no occasion to tell this beautiful child the dread import of her presence there. It seemed a wicked thing to bring her within the range of the trouble.

===Chinese===
玛丽亚·德拉海迟疑了片刻。没有必要让这个美丽的姑娘知道她深夜来此的可怕缘由。把姑娘牵进这场祸事，简直是造孽。

===Original===
“I should like to see Lord Ravenspur, yes,” she said. “So you are his ward, Vera Rayne? Really, I cannot see any likeness between you and your father.”

===Chinese===
“我是想见拉文斯珀勋爵，是的，”她说，“这么说，你就是受他监护的薇拉·雷恩（Vera Rayne）？说真的，我瞧不出你同你父亲有半点相像的地方。”

===Original===
The words had slipped unconsciously from Maria Delahay’s lips before she had time to think what she was saying. It was only when she noted the startled look in Vera’s eyes that she realised the full extent of her imprudent speech.

===Chinese===
玛丽亚·德拉海还没来得及想一想自己在说什么，这些话便不知不觉地溜出了唇边。直到她瞥见薇拉眼中那副惊愕的神色，方才明白这句失言有多冒失。

===Original===
“Did you know my father?” Vera cried.

===Chinese===
“您认识我父亲？”薇拉失声叫道。

===Original===
“What am I saying!” Mrs. Delahay exclaimed. “My head is so dazed and confused that I don’t know what I am talking about. Just for a moment I was filled with a foolish idea that you were Lord Ravenspur’s daughter. It would be strange if you bore a likeness to him, seeing that he is only your guardian.”

===Chinese===
“我这说的是什么呀！”德拉海夫人惊呼道，“我的脑子昏昏沉沉、乱作一团，连自己在讲什么都不知道了。方才我一糊涂，竟冒出个傻念头，以为你是拉文斯珀勋爵的女儿。他不过是你的监护人，你若同他相像，那才怪呢。”

===Original===
Vera was silent for a moment. Mrs. Delahay’s impetuous speech had filled her with misgivings. She did not know, she could not feel sure that, after all, Lord Ravenspur might stand in closer relationship to her than that of a guardian. But she put the trouble out of her mind now. She had other things to occupy her attention. And after all said and done, the poor creature by her side was in deeper grief and anxiety than herself.

===Chinese===
薇拉沉默了片刻。德拉海夫人这番冲口而出的话，在她心里搅起了重重疑虑。她不知道——也无法确信——拉文斯珀勋爵同她的关系，到头来是否可能比监护人还要更近一层。不过此刻她且把这份心事撇开，她还有别的事要操心。再说归根到底，身边这可怜人的悲伤与忧虑，比她自己还要深重。

===Original===
“I think I will go up and tell my guardian you are here,” she said. “I know he will be glad to see you.”

===Chinese===
“我看我还是上楼去，告诉我的监护人您来了，”她说，“我知道他一定乐意见您。”

===Original===
Vera was spared the trouble, for at that moment Ravenspur came into the room with a bundle of papers in his hand. He started as he caught sight of Mrs. Delahay.

===Chinese===
薇拉省了这趟麻烦，因为正在此时，拉文斯珀手里拿着一沓文件走进了房间。他一眼望见德拉海夫人，不由得一惊。

===Original===
“You here at this hour!” he exclaimed. “I hope﻿—”

===Chinese===
“这么晚了您竟在这儿！”他叫道，“我希望——”

===Original===
“No; there is nothing particularly wrong,” the woman said. “I should like a few words with you if I am not intruding.”

===Chinese===
“不；没出什么特别的事，”那妇人说，“若是算不上打扰，我想同您说几句话。”

===Original===
Vera discreetly left the room, and walked off towards the library. There was a stern expression on Ravenspur’s face as he looked at his visitor. He waited for her to speak.

===Chinese===
薇拉识趣地退出房去，朝书房那边走去。拉文斯珀望着来访的客人，脸上是一副冷峻的神情。他等着她开口。

===Original===
“I daresay you will think this is rather singular of me,” she faltered, “but I came here tonight because your life is in danger. I believe that the man called Luigi Silva is under your roof at the present moment. You know who I mean?”

===Chinese===
“您多半会觉得我这样行事未免离奇，”她讷讷地说，“可我今夜到这儿来，是因为您的性命正处在危险之中。我相信，那个名叫路易吉·西尔瓦（Luigi Silva）的男人，此刻就在您的屋檐底下。您知道我指的是谁吧？”

===Original===
“I know perfectly well,” Ravenspur replied. “It would be absurd to pretend to misunderstand you. And so it turns out after all that you are the sister of my poor friend Flavio’s wife. Did Delahay know your identity before he married you?”

===Chinese===
“我一清二楚，”拉文斯珀答道，“再装作听不懂您的话，那就可笑了。这么说，到头来您竟是我那可怜的朋友弗拉维奥（Flavio）的妻子的妹妹。德拉海娶您之前，可知道您的身份？”

===Original===
“He knew all there was that was worth knowing,” the woman said, a little defiantly. “He knew the story of my miserable youth, for instance. I don’t want you to misunderstand me. I don’t wish to pretend that I had any ardent passion for my husband. But my affection was deep and sincere, and my loss is almost more than I can bear. Oh, I know what you are going to say. You are going to ask what I know about that wretched Flavio affair. I repeat in all sincerity that I knew nothing till the other day. I did not even know that my sister was alive, not until I visited her tonight at her house in Isleworth Road. I was not aware that she had married Boris Flavio. I did not know that she had a child﻿—”

===Chinese===
“凡是值得知道的，他全都知道，”那妇人带着几分倔强答道，“譬如说，他就知道我年少时那段凄惨的身世。我不愿让您误会我。我不想假称自己对丈夫怀有多么炽烈的情爱，可我的感情深沉而真挚，痛失亲人，我几乎承受不住。哦，我知道您要说什么。您要问我，对那桩糟心的弗拉维奥一案知道多少。我以全部的诚意再说一遍：直到前几天，我什么都不知道。我甚至不知道我姐姐还活着——直到今夜到艾尔沃思路（Isleworth Road）她家中探望，方才知道。我不知道她嫁给了鲍里斯·弗拉维奥（Boris Flavio）。我也不知道她生过一个孩子——”

===Original===
“Do you know who the child is?” Ravenspur asked swiftly.

===Chinese===
“您可知道那孩子是谁？”拉文斯珀疾声问道。

===Original===
“Yes; I know *now*,” was the significant reply. “I have just been talking to her. What a beautiful girl she is! How sweet and natural! How open and candid is her face! It seems almost incredible to me that my sister could have forgotten her child all these years. I could not have done so.”

===Chinese===
“是的；我*如今*知道了，”这句回答意味深长。“我方才正同她说话呢。多美的姑娘啊！多么甜美，多么自然！那张脸多么开朗，多么坦诚！我姐姐这些年竟能把自己的孩子忘掉，在我看来几乎不可思议。换了我，断然做不到。”

===Original===
“No; nor any other woman worthy of the name,” Ravenspur said grimly. “But though you lived with your sister till early womanhood, you had no real conception of her character. I never met her myself, for which I am devoutly thankful. But I learnt enough, and more than enough, of her character from Flavio’s letters to me. If ever a man was cursed with a fiend incarnate in the shape of a wife, Flavio was that man. Oh, I don’t wish to give you pain, for you have suffered enough of late. But I know what I am talking about. The mere fact that you alluded to just now is proof positive that your sister is incapable of affection for her child. More or less by accident you have made this discovery tonight. By sheer chance you know that your sister’s daughter is under my roof. For a long time past I have known that some agency has been at work to deprive me of the girl, an agency so utterly unscrupulous that my very life is in danger. I suppose that man is acting for your sister, who has a sudden whim to gain possession of her child once more. And now I am going to ask you a favour. You are to say nothing of what you have found out tonight. I have told you what your sister is, and no doubt my words will prove true before long. I am going to ask you to give me a solemn promise that﻿—”

===Chinese===
“不错；凡是配得上‘女人’二字的人，谁也做不到，”拉文斯珀冷冷地说，“然而您虽同姐姐住到成年，对她的人品却并没有真正的认识。我本人从未见过她，为此我深深感谢上苍。不过，从弗拉维奥写给我的信里，我对她的为人知道得够多了，多得过了头。倘若说有哪个男人曾被一个以妻子面目现身的恶魔缠上一生，那便是弗拉维奥。哦，我无意叫您伤心，您近来受的苦已经够多了。可我知道自己说的是什么。方才您提到的那件事本身，便是确凿的证据，证明您姐姐根本不会疼爱自己的孩子。今夜您多半出于偶然，有了这个发现；又全凭机缘凑巧，您知道了您姐姐的女儿就在我家中。很久以来，我便察觉有一股势力在暗中活动，要把这姑娘从我身边夺走，那股势力全无顾忌，连我这条性命都危在旦夕。我料想，那人正是在为您姐姐效力——她一时心血来潮，想把孩子重新弄回自己手中。现在我要请您帮个忙：今夜您探得的一切，一概不可外泄。我已经把您姐姐的为人告诉了您，无疑用不了多久，我的话便会应验。我要请您向我郑重许诺——”

===Original===
“It is too late,” Mrs. Delahay exclaimed. “Whatever my sister may be is all beside the point. She knows where her daughter is, and Luigi Silva knows also. He told us everything not long ago. I found out by accident that he was coming here. I saw him enter the house a few moments ago. I believe he is in your studio at the present moment. That is why I rang the bell so furiously; that is why I prayed I should not be too late.”

===Chinese===
“太迟了，”德拉海夫人叫道，“我姐姐是什么样的人，全都无关紧要。她知道她女儿在哪里，路易吉·西尔瓦也知道。不久前他把一切都告诉了我们。我是碰巧得知他要上这儿来的。几分钟前，我亲眼看见他进了这座宅子。我相信此刻他就在您的画室里。所以我才那样死命地按门铃；所以我才一路祷告，但愿自己来得不算太迟。”

===Original===
Ravenspur started violently.

===Chinese===
拉文斯珀猛然一惊。

===Original===
“Oh, this is intolerable,” he cried. “One could hardly believe it possible that this is London in the twentieth century. I had thought that those insane vendettas had died out before this, even in Corsica. I must go at once and see﻿—”

===Chinese===
“哦，这实在叫人忍无可忍，”他叫道，“简直令人难以相信，这里竟是二十世纪的伦敦。我还当那些疯狂的族间仇杀（vendetta）到如今早已绝迹，连科西嘉（Corsica）也不例外。我必须马上去看看——”

===Original===
As the speaker turned away Maria Delahay held out a detaining hand. Her face was pale and pleading.

===Chinese===
说话人转身欲去，玛丽亚·德拉海伸手拦住了他。她的脸色苍白，满是恳求的神情。

===Original===
“Your life is too valuable to be risked in that headstrong fashion,” she said. “Besides, I have already warned your nephew, who appears to know everything. He went off to the studio at once. I have no doubt that he has scared Silva away by this time. But why don’t you put this matter in the hands of the police? Why run this risk when a few words would prevent any danger? And there need be no scandal. Silva could be warned. He would have to leave the country, and then there would be an end﻿—”

===Chinese===
“您的性命太贵重，不能那样莽撞地去冒险，”她说，“再说，我已经提醒过您侄儿，他好像什么都知道。他当即就到画室去了。这会儿他准已把西尔瓦吓跑了。可您为什么不把这件事交到警察手里？几句话便能杜绝一切危险，何苦还要冒这个险？而且也不至于闹出丑闻。西尔瓦可以受到警告，他不得不离境，这样一来一切便可了结——”

===Original===
“And this from you who are a half Corsican yourself,” Ravenspur said reproachfully. “I could free myself from Silva, no doubt, but before many months had passed another man would take his place and my danger would be greater than ever. You see I have the advantage of knowing my present assailant. To quote the old saying. ‘Better a devil you know than a devil you don’t know.’ ”

===Chinese===
“这样的话竟出自您之口——您自己就有半个科西嘉人的血统，”拉文斯珀语带责备，“摆脱西尔瓦，我无疑办得到，可是不出几个月，便会有另一个人来接替他，我的危险更会甚于以往。您瞧，我占着一样便宜：认得眼下这个要害我的人。套用一句老话说：‘明枪易躲，暗箭难防。’”

===Original===
Maria Delahay had nothing to say in reply. She was turning the matter rapidly over in her mind. It seemed to her that she could see a way out of the difficulty.

===Chinese===
玛丽亚·德拉海无言以对。她心里飞快地盘算着这件事。她觉得，自己仿佛已经看出一条摆脱困境的路子。

===Original===
“I think,” she began, “that perhaps﻿—”

===Chinese===
“我想，”她开口道，“也许——”

===Original===
The words were never finished, for suddenly the tense silence of the house was broken by a quick cry and the tinkling sound of broken glass. Then, in the distance somewhere, a door banged sullenly, and silence fell over the house once more.

===Chinese===
这句话终究没有说完，因为宅子里那绷得紧紧的寂静，骤然被一声急促的呼喊和玻璃碎裂的叮当声打破了。随后，远处不知什么地方，一扇门闷闷地砰然关上，宅子重又归于寂静。

## XXVI A Faithful Servant / 第二十六章 忠仆

===Original===
The words were uttered with a grim coldness that caused Walter to shiver. This was worse than any outbreak of fury, worse than homicidal mania in its most acute form. The man was sane enough beyond all doubt, but, at the same time, he was a fanatic, prepared to gratify his vengeance, even if his own life paid the penalty.

===Chinese===
这些话说得阴森冰冷，沃尔特（Walter）听得不由打了个寒噤。这比任何狂怒的发作都可怖，比最凶险的杀人狂症还可怖。毫无疑问，这人神志极其清醒；可与此同时，他又是个狂热之徒，哪怕要搭上自己的性命，也要一偿复仇之愿。

===Original===
“Well, that is candid, at any rate,” Walter said. “You came here prepared to take my uncle’s life. It was the second attempt that you made upon it. Oh, you know what I mean. You mistook a guest who was coming here for Lord Ravenspur.”

===Chinese===
“哼，至少这话够坦白，”沃尔特说，“你是抱着取我叔父性命的打算到这儿来的。这已经是你第二次对他下手了。哦，你明白我的意思。你把一位将要来此的客人，误认成了拉文斯珀勋爵（Lord Ravenspur）。”

===Original===
“That was a mistake,” Silva said coolly. “It was a mistake that I realised just in time. I should have greatly regretted any harm happening to an innocent party.”

===Chinese===
“那是个误会，”西尔瓦（Silva）冷冷地说，“幸而我及时醒悟了过来。倘使哪个无辜之人因此遭殃，我是会深感遗憾的。”

===Original===
“I suppose it would have quite upset you,” Walter said sarcastically. “But we are wandering from the point. What is the grudge you have against my uncle? You have never even seen him till quite lately. He has been an utter stranger to you.”

===Chinese===
“我想你准会为此难过得不得了吧，”沃尔特讥讽道，“不过我们扯远了。你对我叔父究竟怀有什么仇怨？在此之前，你甚至从未与他谋面，他于你本是个彻头彻尾的陌生人。”

===Original===
A contemptuous smile flickered over Silva’s face.

===Chinese===
一丝轻蔑的笑意在西尔瓦脸上一掠而过。

===Original===
“I don’t suppose I shall be able to make you understand,” he said. “Your race is different to mine. The blood in your veins flows much slower and colder. You have no traditions in this country which are part of your religion. You cannot comprehend that it is one’s duty to avenge insult and outrage, even at the cost of a life. In my part of the world a man would be held a coward who hesitated to retrieve his honour in such a fashion. But in this case it was not my honour, but the honour of the noble house to which I belonged. It would have been bad enough if the thing had been done by one of my own countrymen, but a stranger, like Lord Ravenspur﻿—”

===Chinese===
“我看我是没法叫你明白的，”他说，“你们和我们不是同一种人。你们血管里的血流得慢得多，也冷得多。在你们这个国家，没有任何传统与你们的宗教融为一体、成为信仰的一部分。你们无法理解：洗雪凌辱与暴行乃是一个人的本分，哪怕要以性命作抵。在我来的那个地方，谁若在这等事上迟疑不决、不肯如此挽回自己的名誉，谁就要被众人视作懦夫。可这一回牵涉的还不是我的名誉，而是我所属的那个高贵门第的名誉。这桩事倘若是我的同胞干的，已经够恶劣了，何况是个外来之人，像拉文斯珀勋爵这样的——”

===Original===
“I fail to see the distinction,” Walter murmured.

===Chinese===
“我倒看不出这中间有什么分别，”沃尔特喃喃道。

===Original===
“Ah, that is because you cannot understand. Look you here, signor. I have a mistress to whom I am devotedly attached. I would lay down my life for her. I would do anything to shield her from pain. Let us say that my mistress is married to a man who outwardly possesses all the graces that Nature can bestow. He has the intellectual gifts, too. He is widely beloved and popular wherever he goes. But at heart he is a fiend. The refined cruelties which he uses towards his wife arouse revengeful feelings in my breast, though I dare not gratify them, in case I perish, and leave my beloved mistress in a worse case than ever. But there are others of my clan also serving the noble house from which my mistress came, and they write the Count *the letter*. You don’t know what that means, and I am not going to tell you. But it is the death-warrant, and the Count knows it. He cannot appeal against that. All the forces of the Crown cannot save his life. And then, mysteriously, he dies. But he does not die before he has done one last piece of irreparable mischief. He sees a way to strike his wife to the heart from the other side of the grave. There is a child, perhaps the only thing on earth that the Count loves purely and sincerely. He gets his friend, Lord Ravenspur, to kidnap that child. I tell you if his lordship had come amongst us and dishonoured the threshold of the greatest chief in South Italy he could not have unlocked the floodgates of vengeance in a more thorough manner. Think of the degradation, the bitter insult of it all! If the true facts of the case had been known to me at the time, Lord Ravenspur would have been a dead man years ago. But when my mistress vanished from the world, I naturally thought that she had taken the child with her. I did not know until quite recently what had happened. Then when I cast my mind back to the past I had no difficulty in fixing upon Lord Ravenspur as the culprit. The rest you know.”

===Chinese===
“啊，那是因为你无法理解。你听着，先生。我有一位女主人，我对她一片赤忱，甘愿为她舍弃性命，甘愿做任何事使她免受苦痛。姑且这么说吧：我的女主人嫁给了一个男人，这男人表面上占尽了造化所能赐予的一切风采，又有天赋的才具，无论走到哪里都广受爱戴、深得人心。可骨子里，他是个恶魔。他对妻子使出的种种精巧的虐待手段，在我胸中激起复仇的意念；只是我不敢发作，唯恐自己遭了毒手，撇下心爱的女主人陷于比以往更糟的境地。但我们宗族里还有别的人，也在侍奉我女主人出身的那个高贵门第，是他们给伯爵写了*那封信*。你不懂那意味着什么，我也不打算告诉你。只知道那是一纸死刑判决书，伯爵心里雪亮。他无处申诉。纵然倾尽王室之力，也救不得他的性命。随后，他便神秘地死了。可他在咽气之前，还要干下最后一桩无可挽回的祸事。他寻出一个法子，要从坟墓的那一边，把利刃刺进妻子的心口。世上有了一个孩子——那也许是伯爵在这世上唯一以纯真至诚之心爱着的东西。他便央他的朋友拉文斯珀勋爵，把那孩子劫走了。我告诉你，哪怕勋爵大人当年闯到我们中间，玷污了南意大利最伟大首领的门槛，他把复仇的闸门开得也不能比这更彻底了。想想那是何等的屈辱、何等刻毒的欺凌！倘若当年我知道了这件事的真相，拉文斯珀勋爵早就没命了。可是我的女主人从世间消失后，我自然以为她把孩子带在了身边。直到最近，我才明白究竟出了什么事。待我回首前尘，便毫不费力地认定拉文斯珀勋爵就是那罪魁。其余的事，你都清楚了。”

===Original===
The words were quietly and evenly spoken, but the deep ring of sincerity in them was not lost upon Walter Lance. Here was a man who saw his way clearly before him, a man blinded by prejudice and bigotry, who would not hesitate for a single moment, who would laugh contemptuously at the mere suggestion of personal danger.

===Chinese===
这番话说得平心静气、不疾不徐，但话音里那深沉恳切的真意，沃尔特·兰斯（Walter Lance）听得真切。眼前这个人，把面前的路看得清清楚楚；这是个被偏见与偏执蒙蔽了双眼的人，行事不会有片刻的迟疑，谁若向他提起自身的危险，他只会报以轻蔑的一笑。

===Original===
“What could you do afterwards?” Walter urged.

===Chinese===
“事成之后，你又能怎么样呢？”沃尔特追问道。

===Original===
Silva shrugged his shoulders contemptuously.

===Chinese===
西尔瓦轻蔑地耸了耸肩。

===Original===
“Why go into that?” he said. “The honour of the house would be avenged. I should have done my duty, and have earned the approbation of my friends. There would be a great outcry, no doubt. The thing would be inquired into, and probably the child I speak of would have been restored to her mother, though, to be sure, I am not quite certain whether the Countess is a proper person﻿—”

===Chinese===
“何必细究这个？”他说，“门第的名誉得了昭雪，我便算尽了我的本分，也赢得了我那些朋友的称许。到时候自然免不了掀起一场轩然大波。事情会遭到查究，我所说的那个孩子，多半会被送还她的母亲——虽说老实讲，我拿不准那位伯爵夫人究竟是不是一个合适的人——”

===Original===
“So you have your doubts on that score?” Walter cried eagerly. “Now is it not a fact that the Countess Flavio was notoriously a woman of evil disposition?”

===Chinese===
“这么说，在这一点上你是心存疑虑的了？”沃尔特急切地喊道，“我问你，弗拉维奥伯爵夫人（Countess Flavio）恶名昭著、心肠歹毒，这难道不是事实么？”

===Original===
“Everybody said so,” Silva replied. “Had I chosen, I might have thrown a different light upon it. Mind you, I am not pleased with my late mistress; but there were excuses plausible enough. I cannot forget that it was a horrible thing for a mother to go off and leave her only child all those years. Still, that is no matter. If the time ever came, I could show the world something which would open their eyes as to the doings in his lifetime of Count Flavio. He kept a diary. After his death I found that diary.”

===Chinese===
“人人都是这么说的，”西尔瓦答道，“倘若我愿意，我本可以让世人看到这件事的另一面。你可要明白，我对自己昔日的那位女主人并无好感；但她自有说得过去的苦衷。我只是无法忘记：一个做母亲的竟撇下自己唯一的孩子，一去便是那么多年，这是何等可怕的事。不过，这都无关紧要了。倘有朝一日时机到来，我能拿出一样东西，好叫世人睁开眼睛，看看弗拉维奥伯爵（Count Flavio）生前种种的所作所为。他记着一本日记。他死后，那本日记便到了我的手里。”

===Original===
“And you did not produce it at the trial?”

===Chinese===
“可你在审判的时候没有把它拿出来？”

===Original===
“To what good, signor? Popular prejudice was so strong against us that, beyond doubt, the prosecution would have proved that diary to be a forgery. Then I should have been cast into prison, and my mistress would have been deprived of the one protector whom she so sorely needed. Why, feeling ran so high at the time of the trial that it was dangerous for me to walk the streets alone at night. But why discuss this now? Why continue this unnecessary conversation? You have made up your mind what to do. You have only to ring the bell, and there is an end of me﻿—”

===Chinese===
“拿出来又有什么用，先生？当时世人对于我们一族的偏见是那样强烈，毫无疑问，控方必定会把那本日记论证成伪造之物。到那时我便要被投进监狱，我的女主人也将失去她迫切需要的那唯一的保护人。可不是么，审判那阵子群情汹汹，我夜里独个儿上街都有性命之忧。可如今何必再谈这些？何必把这场不必要的谈话继续下去？你已经拿定了主意。你只消摇一摇铃，我的性命便就此了结——”

===Original===
Silva paused and shrugged his shoulders significantly. He rose as if to take another cigarette. The box slipped from his hand, and some of the little white tubes rolled across the polished floor. With an apology for his clumsiness, he stooped to pick them up. Then he rose again, his right hand shot out in the direction of a figure in armour, grasping a huge battle-axe in its hand. With the swiftness of an animal, the battle-axe was snatched away, and before Walter could realise what had happened, the Italian had smashed a couple of the heavy plate-glass sheets, thus clearing a way into the garden. Walter yelled at the top of his voice and darted forward, but he was too late. He realised the folly of a search in the darkness. No doubt, by this time the man was far away. He opened the studio door, which closed suddenly behind him, owing to a draught which came streaming through the broken panes. He saw Lord Ravenspur standing before him in the corridor, with a white face and agitated manner.

===Chinese===
西尔瓦顿住话头，意味深长地耸了耸肩。他站起身来，像是要再取一支香烟。烟盒从他手中滑落，几支细白的小烟卷滚过光洁的地板。他口称失手，躬身去捡。随即他又直起身来，右手倏地朝一尊甲胄人像探去——那人像手中握着一柄巨大的战斧。只见他出手疾如野兽，转眼间那柄战斧已被夺下；沃尔特还没弄明白出了什么事，那意大利人已经砸碎了两块厚重的平板玻璃，给自己清出一条通向花园的路。沃尔特放声大喊，一个箭步冲上前去，可是太迟了。他明白，在黑暗中搜查无异于徒劳。毫无疑问，此时此刻那人早已远去。他推开画室的门；一阵穿堂风从破碎的窗格里直灌进来，门便忽然在他身后合上了。他看见拉文斯珀勋爵站在走廊里迎着他，脸色惨白，神色仓皇。

===Original===
“What is it, Walter?” the latter asked hoarsely.

===Chinese===
“出什么事了，沃尔特？”勋爵声音沙哑地问。

===Original===
“I’ll tell you presently,” Walter said. “Only you must get the women-kind away first. It is quite imperative that Vera should know nothing, though it doesn’t in the least matter in Mrs. Delahay’s case. She knows all about it.”

===Chinese===
“我回头就告诉你，”沃尔特说，“只是你得先把女眷们打发开。千万不能让薇拉（Vera）知道半点风声；不过德拉海夫人（Mrs. Delahay）那边倒是全然无关紧要，她什么都知道。”

===Original===
Mrs. Delahay, followed by Vera, was in the corridor by this time. The girl’s face was pale. There was an inquiring look in her eyes.

===Chinese===
这时，德拉海夫人已带着薇拉来到走廊里。姑娘脸色苍白，眼中含着探询的神情。

===Original===
“It is really nothing,” Walter said. “Just a little accident on the polished floor of the studio. One of the servants will have to sleep in there tonight in case of intruders. It is a great pity we haven’t got one of the dogs from uncle’s place in Hampshire.”

===Chinese===
“真的没什么，”沃尔特说，“不过是画室光洁的地板上出了点小乱子。今夜得叫一个仆人睡在里头，以防不速之客闯入。可惜我们没能从叔父汉普郡（Hampshire）的宅子里牵一条狗来。”

===Original===
“It is terribly late,” Mrs. Delahay exclaimed, with a significant glance in Walter’s direction. “Really, I ought to be back at my hotel long ago. I suppose I can find a cab?”

===Chinese===
“实在太晚啦，”德拉海夫人叫道，意味深长地朝沃尔特那边瞟了一眼，“说真的，我早该回旅馆去了。我想我总能叫到一辆马车吧？”

===Original===
“I will go and find one for you,” Walter said. “Hadn’t you better go to bed, Vera? Don’t forget that you are likely to be up very late tomorrow night.”

===Chinese===
“我去替你叫一辆，”沃尔特说，“薇拉，你还是去睡吧，好不好？别忘了，明晚你恐怕要熬到很晚呢。”

===Original===
Very few words sufficed to tell Mrs. Delahay what had happened.

===Chinese===
只消寥寥数语，便足以让德拉海夫人明白出了什么事。

===Original===
Walter saw her into a cab, after which he returned to the house. He was relieved to find that Vera had already retired. Lord Ravenspur was walking moodily up and down the library.

===Chinese===
沃尔特送她上了马车，随后回到宅中。得知薇拉已经回房安歇，他松了一口气。拉文斯珀勋爵正在书房里郁郁地来回踱步。

===Original===
“One of the grooms is going to sleep in the studio,” he said. “We can get the damage repaired tomorrow. And now tell me everything. I am certain that you have something unpleasant to disclose.”

===Chinese===
“有一个马夫今晚睡在画室里，”他说，“损坏之处明天便可修好。现在，把一切都讲给我听吧。我敢肯定，你有什么不痛快的事情要透露。”

===Original===
Walter told his story at some length. Lord Ravenspur followed with every sign of interest.

===Chinese===
沃尔特把事情细细讲了一遍，讲得颇为详尽。拉文斯珀勋爵全神贯注地听着，神情间处处透着关切。

===Original===
“Oh, I can quite understand that man’s point of view,” he said. “You see, I know something about those people. When I was quite a young man I spent a year or two in Corsica, and, to a certain extent, I sympathise with them. I have committed an outrage on the national honour, and I am to pay the penalty with my life. The thing is recognised out there. It is regarded as quite commonplace.”

===Chinese===
“哦，那人的看法，我是完全能够理解的，”他说，“你要知道，我对那一类人多少有些了解。我年轻的时候，在科西嘉（Corsica）住过一两年，在某种程度上，我是同情他们的。我冒犯了他们的民族荣誉，就该拿性命来抵偿。在那边，这种事是得到公认的，人们把它看作寻常之事。”

===Original===
“And there is no way of clearing yourself?” Walter asked.

===Chinese===
“那你就没有办法洗刷自己么？”沃尔特问。

===Original===
“Well, perhaps there is one,” Ravenspur said thoughtfully. “You see, the head of the family can interfere. Vera’s mother is in a position to say﻿—But what am I talking about? My dear boy, my life is in danger, and I am afraid that even if we lay Silva by the heels there will be others. But, come what may, I am going to ask for no clemency. Come what may, Vera shall never pass into the custody of that vile woman whom she has the misfortune to call her mother. Nothing shall induce me to change my mind. Indeed, such a thing would be a violation of my promise to the dead.”

===Chinese===
“嗯，办法或许有一个，”拉文斯珀沉吟着说，“你要知道，家族的首领是可以出面干预的。薇拉的母亲便有资格发话——可我在说些什么呀？亲爱的孩子，我的性命正在危险之中，而且恐怕即便我们擒住了西尔瓦，还会有别的人接踵而来。但无论如何，我决不乞求宽赦。无论如何，薇拉决不能交到那个她不幸须称作母亲的卑劣女人手里去。什么也不能使我改变主意。真的，那样做便违背了我对亡者的许诺。”

===Original===
“Your sentiments do you honour,” Walter said; “but, unhappily, I don’t see how you can carry them out.”

===Chinese===
“你这份心志可敬可佩，”沃尔特说，“只可惜，我看不出你有什么法子把它付诸实行。”

## XXXI A Missing Link / 第三十一章 缺失的一环

===Original===
It was practically daylight now, so that the greatest caution was absolutely necessary. It was possible to obtain cover behind a group of thorn bushes and take observations of the house. But even that did not lack risk, all the more so because of the presence of the dog. The great hound had served his purpose, and it was essential that he should be got rid of, for the present at any rate.

===Chinese===
天色实际上已经大亮，因此务必加倍小心才行。他们可以躲到一丛荆棘后面，观察那座房子。但即便如此也并非没有风险，更何况还有那条狗在。这头大猎犬已经尽了它的用场，总之必须先设法摆脱它，至少眼下是这样。

===Original===
The house itself was quite a good one. The grounds were neat and trim. The flower boxes in the windows ablaze with bloom. The place spoke for itself as the residence of some prosperous individual who, in all probability, was somebody of importance in the City. It was the last place in the world to associate with crime and violence. In front of the house was a fairly large lawn, shaded by shrubs and trees. A kitchen garden at the back was bounded by a lane, and on the far side of this stretched a wide open common covered with gorse and bracken.

===Chinese===
这座房子本身就相当不错。庭院收拾得干净齐整，窗台上的花箱里鲜花盛开、灼灼耀眼。这地方本身便足以说明，它是某位殷实人物的居所，十有八九还是伦敦城里有头有脸的角色。要说它与罪案暴行有什么干系，那可是天下最不相称的事。房前是一片相当大的草坪，掩映在灌木与树荫之下。屋后有一片菜园，园子尽头傍着一条小巷，小巷再往外，便是一片开阔的公地，长满了荆豆和蕨草。

===Original===
“Have you any idea where we are?” Lord Ravenspur asked.

===Chinese===
“你知不知道我们现在是在什么地方？”拉文斯珀勋爵（Lord Ravenspur）问道。

===Original===
“Not the faintest,” Walter confessed. “I have never been here before. The only thing I am sure of is that Vera is in yonder house. But let us get away from here and talk it over. The further this thing goes, the more sure I am that we have foes to deal with who are clever as they are unscrupulous.”

===Chinese===
“丝毫不知，”沃尔特（Walter）坦白承认，“我从前从来没有到过这里。我唯一能肯定的是，薇拉（Vera）就在那边那座房子里。不过，我们先离开这儿，再从长计议。这件事越往下深究，我越确信，我们打交道的这些仇敌，既狡猾又肆无忌惮。”

===Original===
“But where shall we go?” Ravenspur asked.

===Chinese===
“可我们上哪儿去呢？”拉文斯珀问。

===Original===
Walter suggested skirting round the back of the house, and so on to the common. Once there, they found shelter enough, for the gorse was high and the bracken was deep. Indeed, a regiment of soldiers might have hidden there with perfect safety.

===Chinese===
沃尔特提议绕到房子后面，再到那片公地上去。一到那里，他们便发现藏身之处绰绰有余，因为荆豆长得高，蕨草长得深。说实在的，哪怕藏下整整一个团的士兵，也万无一失。

===Original===
“I think I begin to see my way,” Walter said. “One of us must stay here and the other get back to London without delay. If you don’t mind, I should like to consult my friend Venables about this business. He is very clever and courageous, and, besides, he has a decided fondness for detective business. I think you will agree with me that we want another hand?”

===Chinese===
“我想我渐渐摸到门路了，”沃尔特说，“咱们俩得有一个留在这里，另一个马上赶回伦敦去。你若不介意，我想就这件事找我的朋友维纳布斯（Venables）商量商量。他这人又聪明又有胆量，况且天生酷爱侦探这一行。我想你会同意我的：我们需要再添个帮手？”

===Original===
“We want half a dozen,” Ravenspur murmured. “What we really ought to do is to place the matter in the hands of the police and have yonder house searched at once. Yet, I am very loth to do that. I am exceedingly anxious to prevent anything like a scandal, and this is the very sort of thing to appeal to the cheap Press. But what are we going to do about our journey to Weymouth? What would the officials at Waterloo think when we didn’t turn up last night? And, again, there are all the servants in Park Lane. If you can only show me some way to stop the mouths of these people I shall be grateful. You know what servants are.”

===Chinese===
“要我说，需要六个才够，”拉文斯珀咕哝道，“我们真正该做的，是把这件事交到警察手里，立刻搜查那边那座房子。可是我又极不愿意这样做。我万分担心闹出什么丑闻来，而这种事恰恰最对低俗小报的胃口。再说，我们去韦茅斯（Weymouth）的那趟行程怎么办？昨天夜里我们没有在滑铁卢车站（Waterloo）露面，站上的职员们会怎么想？还有，帕克巷（Park Lane）宅子里那一众仆人。你要是能指给我一条封住这些人嘴巴的法子，我就感激不尽了。你是知道仆人的。”

===Original===
“I think that can be managed,” Walter said after a thoughtful pause. “You stay here while I go back to London. I will return as soon as possible. Oh, of course, I will bring a change of clothing with me. It would be madness to hang about a suburb like this in evening dress. We should be spotted in a moment.”

===Chinese===
“我想这事还能应付下来，”沃尔特沉吟片刻，说道，“你留在这里，我回伦敦去一趟，尽快赶回来。哦，对了，我当然会随身带一套换洗的衣物来。穿着一身晚礼服在这种郊区地方晃荡，简直是发疯——我们转眼就会被人认出来。”

===Original===
It seemed to Ravenspur that there was no help for it. Anxious and troubled and worn out as he was, he could not be altogether blind to the absurdity of the situation. The idea of a man in his position hiding himself on a London common, dressed as he was, seemed ridiculous. He had no more than a dust coat over his evening dress; he was wearing the collar of an Order. Still, as he looked about him he took fresh heart of grace. The common appeared to be little frequented. There were deep hollows here and there, full of bracken and brambles, under which it was possible to hide. There was no prospect of Walter getting back within the next three hours. There was nothing for it but to make the best of the situation.

===Chinese===
在拉文斯珀看来，除此以外也别无他法。他虽然忧心忡忡、烦恼缠身、疲惫不堪，却也不能全然看不见眼下处境的荒唐。像他这样身份的人物，穿着这样一身行头，藏身于伦敦的一片公地之上——这念头实在可笑。他晚礼服外面只罩了一件防尘外衣，颈间还挂着勋位领章。不过，他环顾四周，重新鼓起了勇气。这片公地似乎少有人来，到处都有深深的洼地，长满蕨草与荆棘，正好藏身。沃尔特三个钟头以内是回不来的，他也只能善处眼前的局面了。

===Original===
Meanwhile, Walter was hurrying back to London. He made a wide detour of the common, so that it was not possible for him to be seen from the house. Then presently he struck a main road on the far side of which ran a railway line. He could see in the distance the buildings and signals that marked a station. At any rate, he would be able to find out where he was without displaying his ignorance by asking questions. It was still quite early, only a little past five o’clock, as Walter found on consulting his watch. After all said and done, the station was not much use to him, for probably no train would run within the next couple of hours. Presently there was a clatter of hoofs behind, and an empty hansom came along. The sight of the cab was proof to Walter that he was not very far outside the radius. A happy idea came to him.

===Chinese===
这时，沃尔特正匆匆赶回伦敦。他绕着公地兜了个大圈子，免得被那座房子里的人望见。随后不久，他踏上了一条大路，路的那一侧顺着一条铁路。远处可以望见房屋和信号机，标示着一座车站的位置。这样一来，他至少能弄清自己身在何处，又不必开口问路、露出无知的样子。看看怀表，天色还早，刚过五点。说到底，车站对他用处不大，因为一两个钟头之内恐怕不会有火车通行。不多时，身后传来一阵得得的马蹄声，一辆空着的汉森式双轮马车驶了过来。沃尔特一看这马车，便明白自己离市区马车的营业范围已不很远。他灵机一动，有了主意。

===Original===
“Are you going back to town?” he asked the cabman.

===Chinese===
“你要回城里去吗？”他问马车夫。

===Original===
“Well, yes, sir,” the cabman explained. “I have been taking a fare out to Cannon Green.”

===Chinese===
“嗯，正是回城去，先生，”马车夫答道，“我刚送了一位客人到卡农格林（Cannon Green）来。”

===Original===
“Then you are just the man for me,” Walter exclaimed. “My man has failed to turn up, and I was going to try the station. I suppose that is Cannon Green station just at the end of the road?”

===Chinese===
“那你可正是我要找的人，”沃尔特喜出望外，“我的仆人失约没来，我正打算到车站去碰碰运气。大路尽头那边，想必就是卡农格林车站吧？”

===Original===
“That’s right, sir,” the cabman said civilly. “But you’ll get no train yet. Drive you anywhere you like, sir, for half-a-sovereign.”

===Chinese===
“没错，先生，”马车夫客客气气地答道，“可眼下还没有火车呢。只要半镑金币，先生，您想去哪儿，我就送您去哪儿。”

===Original===
Walter jumped into the cab without further hesitation. A ride of a little over an hour brought him to Park Lane. A sleepy footman opened the door, and regarded Walter in amazement. He had his story all ready. There had been misunderstanding on the previous evening, and Lord Ravenspur and Miss Rayne had gone on to Weymouth by an early train. There was something very paltry about this deception, but at the same time it seemed to Walter to be absolutely necessary. He roused his own man; together they packed a couple of portmanteaux, which Walter gave directions should be taken to Waterloo Station without delay, and left in the cloakroom. Once he had satisfied the curiosity of the household in Park Lane, he went on promptly to Venables’ rooms. Over a hasty breakfast he explained everything that had happened to his companion. As he expected, Venables at once threw himself heart and soul into the adventure.

===Chinese===
沃尔特不再迟疑，一跃上了车。一个钟头多一点的车程，把他送到了帕克巷。一个睡眼惺忪的男仆开了门，一见沃尔特，满面惊愕。他的说辞早已编好：头天晚上出了点误会，拉文斯珀勋爵和雷恩小姐（Miss Rayne）已经乘早班火车先赴韦茅斯了。这套骗术实在蹩脚得很，可沃尔特同时又觉得非此不可。他叫醒了自己带来的仆人，两人一起收拾了两只旅行皮箱；沃尔特吩咐立刻把箱子送往滑铁卢车站，寄放在行李房里。把帕克巷宅中众人的好奇心应付过去之后，他便径直前往维纳布斯的寓所。一顿匆匆的早餐之间，他把发生的一切向他的伙伴和盘托出。不出所料，维纳布斯当即全心全意地投进了这场冒险。

===Original===
“I quite understand your point of view,” he exclaimed. “What you want to do is to hang about all day and take observations. At the same time, it is absolutely necessary that we should arouse no suspicions. I think I can see my way. This is a matter of disguise. We can pick up all we want in this direction in Covent Garden on our way to the station.”

===Chinese===
“你的处境我完全明白，”他大声道，“你们要做的，是一整天守在附近暗中观察。与此同时，我们务必不可引起半点怀疑。我想我有法子了。这就要靠化装。去车站的路上，我们可以在科文特花园（Covent Garden）把这一路要用的东西统统置办齐备。”

===Original===
“There is only one thing that worries me,” Walter said, “and that is Bruno. What shall we do with him?”

===Chinese===
“只有一件事叫我放心不下，”沃尔特说，“那就是布鲁诺（Bruno）。我们拿它怎么办？”

===Original===
“Oh, that’s all right,” Venables cried. “Very well-trained dog, isn’t he? So much the better. You see, in the course of my adventures I have come across some pretty shady specimens of humanity, though I am bound to say that I have found many of this class pretty faithful when they are well paid. Now I know a fellow at Cannon Green who will look after the dog for us for a consideration. He is a fancier himself, and always has a few animals for sale. What more natural than that he should have a bloodhound on the premises? Mr. Bill Perks is more than suspected of being a receiver of stolen goods, and on two occasions has been in trouble. Still, he knows me, and will do anything I like, provided I pay him handsomely. Don’t let us waste any more time.”

===Chinese===
“哦，那不成问题，”维纳布斯嚷道，“它是条训练有素的狗，对不对？那就更好了。要知道，我历次冒险，什么不三不四的人都见识过；不过我得说句公道话，这一路人等里头，只要钱给得足，倒有不少是相当靠得住的。我正好认识卡农格林的一个人，给他一笔钱，他就会替我们照看这条狗。他自己就是个贩狗的行家，手头总有几只牲口出卖。那么，他场院里再养着一条寻血猎犬，还有比这更顺理成章的事吗？比尔·珀克斯先生（Bill Perks）几乎可以断定是个收赃的，而且还两度吃过官司。不过他认得我，只要我钱给得漂亮，让他干什么他都肯。咱们别再浪费时间了。”

===Original===
An hour later and the two adventurers left the train at Cannon Green, bearing a set of instruments such as those used by surveyors when they are planning and laying out new land. Their disguise was slight enough, but quite sufficient for the purpose. Both wore smart looking caps, edged with gold, so that their appearance was sufficiently formal and official. In addition to this Walter carried a bulky bag, which contained a complete change of clothing for Lord Ravenspur. The latter was glad enough to see Walter and Venables. He breathed a little more freely when he found himself clad at length in a Norfolk suit. He rather rebelled against a showy white helmet and a set of long grey whiskers which Venables proceeded to attach dexterously to his face. A few touches of grease paint and pencil, together with a pair of big spectacles, rendered the disguise complete. Walter expressed his admiration.

===Chinese===
一个钟头以后，两位冒险家在卡农格林下了火车，携带着一整套测量员规划新地时所用的仪器。他们的化装够轻简的，却也足以奏效。两人都戴着镶金边的漂亮帽子，看上去足够体面、足够有官派。除此之外，沃尔特还提着一只鼓鼓囊囊的大提包，里面装着给拉文斯珀勋爵从头到脚的一套换洗衣物。见到沃尔特和维纳布斯，勋爵高兴极了。待终于换上一套诺福克套装，他才略略松了口气。只是对那顶扎眼的白色头盔，以及维纳布斯随即熟练地替他贴到脸上的那副长长的灰色络腮胡，他颇有些抗拒。再抹上几笔油彩、描上几道铅笔痕，配上一副大眼镜，这化装便大功告成。沃尔特连声赞叹。

===Original===
“I can hardly believe that it is you, uncle,” he said.

===Chinese===
“我简直不敢相信这会是您，叔父，”他说。

===Original===
“And you are changed almost out of recognition,” Ravenspur said. “Really, I must compliment Mr. Venables. And now, I suppose I had better hide the bag in these bushes. We have a very anxious day before us, I am afraid, but that does not prevent one feeling the call of Nature. I don’t think I was ever so ravenously hungry in my life. Where can we get some breakfast?”

===Chinese===
“你也变得几乎认不出来了，”拉文斯珀说，“说真的，我得好好称道一番维纳布斯先生。现在，我想我还是把这只包藏进这些灌木丛里去吧。恐怕我们面前摆着的是忧心如焚的一天，可这也挡不住大自然的召唤——我想我这辈子还从来没有饿得这样凶过。我们上哪儿能吃上一顿早饭呢？”

===Original===
Venables, who seemed to have thought of everything, had already solved the problem. There was a very fair hotel not far from the station, and it would be an easy matter to hide the surveying outfit till it was needed. In the first place, they could skirt round the edge of the common, and pay a call on the man Perks, who would look after the dog till his services were needed again.

===Chinese===
维纳布斯似乎把什么都想到了，早已把这个问题解决妥当。离车站不远有一家相当不错的旅馆，把那套测量器械先藏起来、等要用时再取，也不是什么难事。眼下他们可以先沿着公地边缘绕行，去拜访一下珀克斯其人，那条狗就托付给他照看，直到再需要它出力为止。

===Original===
They came at length to Perks’ house, a rather dilapidated looking place, with a large, untidy garden around it. There were sheds and huts and kennels at the back, so that the intruders were greeted with a terrible din of barking as they went up the path.

===Chinese===
他们终于来到珀克斯的住处——一座样子相当破败的房子，四周围着一个凌乱的大园子。屋后搭着棚屋、小舍和一排排狗窝，几位来客刚走上小径，一片震耳的犬吠便劈头盖脸地迎了上来。

===Original===
“Cunning hand, Perks,” Venables explained. “It is very little he makes out of dog-fancying. But see how useful these animals are. Day or night the police can never approach the place with the object of raid without Perks having ample notice. But come along, and you shall see the man for yourselves. Oh, you needn’t trouble yourself about your disguise. Perks is not in the least suspicious nor will he ask any questions. He will only think that you are a couple of amateur detectives like myself.”

===Chinese===
“珀克斯这家伙，滑头得很，”维纳布斯解释道，“单靠贩狗，他其实捞不了几个钱。可你瞧瞧这些畜生多么有用。无论白天黑夜，警察想突击搜查这个地方，不等靠近，珀克斯就早有充分的风声了。好了，跟我来，让你们亲眼见见这个人。哦，你们不必为化装的事操心。珀克斯丝毫不会起疑，也不会问长问短。他只会把你们当成跟我一样的两个业余侦探罢了。”

## XLI A Ray of Light / 第四十一章 一线光明

===Original===
It was no difficult matter to find Mrs. Delahay, who, when the late startling developments were laid before her, made no demur in giving her sister’s address. Maria Delahay was looking just as pale and haggard as usual. It seemed impossible to rouse her from the state of apathy into which she had fallen. When the two friends were standing on the steps of the hotel they ran against Inspector Dallas.

===Chinese===
找到德拉海夫人并不难。有人把近来这桩桩骇人的变故摆到她面前，她毫不推拒，说出了妹妹的住址。玛丽亚·德拉海（Maria Delahay）看上去还是一如既往地苍白憔悴，似乎怎么也没法把她从那消沉麻木的状态里唤醒过来。两位朋友正站在饭店门前的台阶上，迎面撞见了达拉斯探长（Inspector Dallas）。

===Original===
“Any fresh news?” Walter asked.

===Chinese===
“有什么新的消息吗？”沃尔特（Walter）问。

===Original===
“Well, no,” Dallas admitted. “I am simply waiting on events at present. If you could only get Mrs. Delahay to be more candid with me it might save her a deal of unpleasantness in the long run.”

===Chinese===
“呃，没有，”达拉斯坦然承认，“眼下我只是静观其变。要是你们能让德拉海夫人（Mrs. Delahay）对我坦白一些，从长远看，说不定能替她省去许多不快。”

===Original===
“Do you mean that she is in danger?” Venables asked.

===Chinese===
“你是说她处境危险？”维纳布斯（Venables）问。

===Original===
“I think you can see that for yourself, sir,” Dallas replied. “Naturally, I am hesitating as long as possible﻿—”

===Chinese===
“先生，我想这一点您自己也能看出来，”达拉斯答道，“自然，我正尽可能拖延着——”

===Original===
“Then hesitate a little longer,” Walter said. “Quite by accident Lord Ravenspur has stumbled upon a clue which I think will astonish you when you come to know his story. We are going off now to a place called Cannon Green. We shall probably be back by the last train tonight, and I want you to meet us at Waterloo Station. I think you will be well rewarded for your trouble.”

===Chinese===
“那就再拖延一阵吧，”沃尔特说，“拉文斯珀勋爵（Lord Ravenspur）纯属偶然撞见了一条线索，等你听完他的经过，我想你会大吃一惊的。我们现在就去一个叫卡农格林（Cannon Green）的地方，今晚多半乘末班车回来，请你到滑铁卢车站（Waterloo Station）接我们一趟。我想，你这番周折不会白费。”

===Original===
Dallas promised, and, like the wise man he was, asked no questions. The two companions proceeded in a cab to Isleworth Road, where they asked to see the mistress of the house. The maid who answered the door was somewhat reticent, but she admitted that her mistress was at home, whereupon Walter and his companion entered without further ceremony. Perhaps their manner impressed the maid, for she came back a moment later saying that her mistress would see the visitors. The Countess entered the drawing-room and glanced with cold displeasure at the intruders.

===Chinese===
达拉斯应承下来，并且不愧是个明白人，一句也不多问。两个同伴乘上马车，前往艾尔沃思路（Isleworth Road），到了那里便求见宅子的女主人。应门的女仆言语有些支吾，但承认女主人确实在家，于是沃尔特和同伴也不再多礼，径直走了进去。多半是他们的气派镇住了女仆，片刻之后她回来说，女主人愿意见客。伯爵夫人走进客厅，冷冷地、满含不悦地扫了这两个不速之客一眼。

===Original===
“What can you possibly want with me?” she demanded.

===Chinese===
“你们找我究竟有什么事？”她质问道。

===Original===
“Perhaps I had better explain,” Walter began. “My name is Lance, and I am a nephew of Lord Ravenspur. Lord Ravenspur has a ward who is called Miss Vera Rayne. In other words, I understand that Miss Rayne is really your daughter.”

===Chinese===
“还是由我先说明一下吧，”沃尔特开口道，“我姓兰斯（Lance），是拉文斯珀勋爵的侄子。拉文斯珀勋爵有位受监护人，名叫薇拉·雷恩（Vera Rayne）小姐。换句话说，据我所知，这位雷恩小姐其实是您的女儿。”

===Original===
The Countess’s cold face flushed slightly.

===Chinese===
伯爵夫人那张冷冰冰的脸上微微泛起红晕。

===Original===
“I am not prepared to contradict you,” she said.

===Chinese===
“我不打算否认，”她说。

===Original===
“My dear madam,” Walter said, “this is no time for diplomacy. Rightly or wrongly, my uncle came to Italy eighteen years ago and kidnapped your daughter. You see, I am quite candid, and I hope you will be good enough to be as candid in your replies. My uncle will himself explain why he took this bold step, but I understand that your late husband did not consider you a desirable parent for a child, and he made my uncle promise to remove the child from your influence. Into the morality of that question I am not disposed to go. For nearly eighteen years nothing happened, and my uncle began to regard Vera quite as his own child. Then the truth came out, and some emissary of yours came to England, prepared to go to any length to regain possession of your daughter. I need not say I am alluding to the man called Silva, also known to many people as Valdo, the flying man. This servant of yours made no fewer than three attacks on my uncle’s life, none of which, fortunately, was successful. And then, I understand, you came on the scene. I believe you were instrumental in luring your daughter from Lady Ringmar’s the night before last, and getting her imprisoned at a place called Cannon Green. One moment, please. I would not deny it, if I were you﻿—”

===Chinese===
“亲爱的夫人，”沃尔特说，“现在可不是耍外交辞令的时候。十八年前，我叔父去到意大利，不管是非对错，把您的女儿劫走了。您瞧，我相当坦白，还望您赏个脸，回答得同样坦白。他为何迈出这一大胆的步骤，我叔父自会亲自解释；不过据我所知，您已故的丈夫认为您不是个宜于抚养孩子的人，于是要我叔父答应把孩子从您身边带走。这件事的是非曲直，我不打算深究。此后将近十八年，风平浪静，我叔父渐渐把薇拉（Vera）当成了自己的亲生女儿。后来真相败露，您派出的密使来到英国，为了重新夺回您的女儿，什么都干得出来。不必说，我指的就是那个名叫西尔瓦（Silva）的人，也就是许多人熟知的‘飞人’瓦尔多（Valdo）。您这位仆人对我叔父的性命下毒手足有三次，所幸一次也没有得逞。再后来，据我了解，您亲自出场了。我相信，前天夜里把您女儿从林格马夫人（Lady Ringmar）家里诱出、把她囚在一个叫卡农格林的地方，您是出了大力的。请稍等。换了我，就不会否认——”
===Original===
“I am not going to deny it,” the Countess said in a hard, dry voice. “There is no occasion to.”

===Chinese===
“我本来就不打算否认，”伯爵夫人声音干硬地说道，“也没有否认的必要。”

===Original===
“Ah, well, that being so, we shall get on all the better. Directly we discovered what had happened we set off in pursuit, fortunately aided by a bloodhound of my uncle’s, who had followed us to Lady Ringmar’s from Park Lane. To make a long story short, we broke into the house, and Miss Rayne is once more under the protection of Lord Ravenspur. But your man, Silva, does not lack resource, and he managed to drug me and drag me out into the garden. Unfortunately for him, the dog was prowling about, and, knowing me and recognising my peril, he made a furious attack upon Silva, with the result that your friend lies in a critical condition and is not expected to live. After what I have told you, I think you will see the necessity of coming down to Cannon Green with us without delay.”

===Chinese===
“啊，好，既然这样，事情就好办多了。我们一发现出了事，立刻动身追赶，幸亏有我叔父养的一条寻血猎犬相助——它是跟我们从帕克巷（Park Lane）一路到林格马夫人家的。长话短说，我们闯进了那座宅子，雷恩小姐重新回到了拉文斯珀勋爵的护佑之下。可是您那位西尔瓦并非省油的灯，他设法把我麻醉了，把我拖到花园里。对他来说倒霉的是，那条狗正在附近逡巡；它认得我，也看出我处境危急，便朝西尔瓦猛扑过去，结果您这位朋友如今性命垂危，眼看是活不成了。听了我这番话，我想您会明白，有必要立即随我们一道去一趟卡农格林。”

===Original===
During this recital the Countess made no sign. She listened with a calmness and unconcern which moved Walter to anger. After all, whatever Silva’s faults might have been, his devotion to his mistress left nothing to be desired. The Countess sat thoughtfully for a few moments before she replied.

===Chinese===
在这一番陈述中，伯爵夫人不动声色。她听着，那份平静与漠然，惹得沃尔特心头火起。毕竟，西尔瓦纵有千般不是，他对女主人的忠心却无可挑剔。伯爵夫人若有所思地坐了片刻，方才开口。

===Original===
“I think I see what you mean,” she said presently. “You want as far as possible to avoid a scandal?”

===Chinese===
“我想我明白你的意思了，”她终于说道，“你们是想尽量避免一桩丑闻，对不对？”

===Original===
“Well, naturally,” Walter said warmly. “In your daughter’s interests it is your duty to assist us. If you fall in line with this idea, the general public will be none the wiser. And when you come to know what manner of man it is that your servant has been attempting to murder in absolutely cold blood﻿—”

===Chinese===
“这个自然，”沃尔特恳切地说，“为了您女儿的缘故，您有义务协助我们。只要您顺着这个意思行事，外界便丝毫不会察觉。等您知道了您那位仆人一直冷血到极点、蓄意谋害的究竟是怎样一个人——”

===Original===
“Oh, I know what manner of man he is,” the Countess cried. “He is the same manner of man as my husband. And a more cold-blooded scoundrel never drew the breath of life. But make no mistake about one thing﻿—I was a party to no violence. All I wanted was to have my child back again, and I hoped that when once this was done, I should be able to induce Silva to forego the vengeance which to him was a part of his religion. You will understand presently why I have appeared to act so strangely. Not but what Lord Ravenspur deserved whatever fate he got at the hands of Silva. Still, we are wasting time in talking like this. I am ready to come with you to Cannon Green at once, more especially because you are right in saying that it is my duty to try and avoid anything in the shape of a scandal. If you will give me five minutes and call a cab, I am absolutely at your service.”

===Chinese===
“哦，他是怎样一个人，我清楚得很，”伯爵夫人叫道，“他跟我丈夫是同一路人，天底下再没有比他更冷血的恶棍了。不过有一件事请不要弄错——我绝没有参与任何暴力。我想要的只是把我的孩子要回来，而且我一直指望，这事一旦办成，我就能劝西尔瓦放弃那桩在他看来如同教义一般的复仇。至于我为什么行事如此古怪，你们片刻之后就明白了。话虽如此，拉文斯珀勋爵落在西尔瓦手里遭到什么命运，也都是他应得的。不过，再这样谈下去就是浪费时间了。我这就跟你们去卡农格林，尤其因为你说得对：尽力避免任何形式的丑闻，是我的本分。请给我五分钟，再去叫一辆马车，我完全听候你们的差遣。”

===Original===
It was a little before five when this strangely assorted group reached Cannon Green. The doctor was just coming away, and Walter asked eagerly after the patient.

===Chinese===
五点差一点儿的时候，这群形形色色的怪组合到了卡农格林。医生正从里面出来，沃尔特急切地打听病人的情况。

===Original===
“Oh, practically he is no better,” the medical man explained. “I mean, he isn’t going to get well. Just for the present he is buoyed up with a strong stimulant, and is in full possession of his faculties. He seems to want something, but I can’t make out what it is. We gave him a sheet of paper and a pencil just now, and he scribbled a word or two, which, being Italian, we could not make out.”

===Chinese===
“哦，实际上他毫无起色，”医生解释道，“我是说，他好不了了。眼下全凭强效兴奋剂撑着，神志还完全清醒。他似乎想要什么，可我猜不出是什么。刚才我们给了他一张纸、一支铅笔，他涂写了寥寥数词，因为是意大利文，我们认不出来。”

===Original===
“I think I know what he wants,” Walter said. “May I suggest, Countess, that you go up to the poor man’s bedroom at once?”

===Chinese===
“我想我知道他要什么，”沃尔特说，“伯爵夫人，可否请您立刻上楼，到那可怜人的房间里去？”

===Original===
Silva’s face lighted up as his eyes fell upon his mistress. He pointed to the bandages about his throat. His lips moved, but no sound came from them.

===Chinese===
西尔瓦一眼望见自己的女主人，脸上顿时亮了起来。他指了指喉部缠着的绷带，嘴唇翕动，却发不出半点声音。

===Original===
“I know exactly what has happened,” the Countess said. “No, pray don’t distress yourself. You must not try your strength. You will never get better if you exert yourself.”

===Chinese===
“出了什么事，我一清二楚，”伯爵夫人说，“不，请千万别焦心。你可不能试探气力。若强行劳神，你永远好不了。”
===Original===
A melancholy smile came over Silva’s face. The expression of his eyes told as plainly as possible that he had no delusions on the score of his recovery. Then he went through the motion of writing with an imaginary pencil upon an invisible paper. Countess Flavio turned impulsively to the nurse.

===Chinese===
一丝凄凉的笑意掠过西尔瓦的脸。他的眼神再明白不过地表明，对于自己能否痊愈，他不抱任何幻想。随后，他做出写字的动作，仿佛用一支想象的铅笔，写在一张看不见的纸上。弗拉维奥伯爵夫人（Countess Flavio）情不自禁地转向护士。

===Original===
“Is it quite safe?” she asked. “I don’t think the poor fellow will rest till he makes me understand; and you see, being Italian myself, anything he may write﻿—”

===Chinese===
“这样做稳妥吗？”她问，“依我看，这可怜人不让我明白他的意思，是绝不会安生的；再说，您瞧，我自己就是意大利人，他写下的任何东西——”

===Original===
“I think it will be a very good thing,” the nurse replied.

===Chinese===
“我看这倒是大好事，”护士答道。

===Original===
She came to the bedside with a sheet of paper and a pencil, which she placed in Silva’s hand. His unsteady fingers began to trace certain signs on the paper. The marks were feeble and straggling enough, but a little care on the part of the Countess enabled her to make out what the characters represented.

===Chinese===
她拿着一张纸和一支铅笔来到床边，把它们放到西尔瓦手里。他颤巍巍的手指开始在纸上描出一些字迹。那些笔画微弱零乱，但伯爵夫人稍加辨认，便认出了那些字符写的是什么。

===Original===
“It is quite plain to me now,” she said, looking down into Silva’s eager face. “You want me to find the diary, do you not? You mean the Count’s diary, which was not produced at the trial?”

===Chinese===
“现在我完全看明白了，”她低头望着西尔瓦急切的脸，说道，“你要我去找那本日记，是不是？你说的是伯爵的日记，开庭审判时没有出示的那本？”

===Original===
Silva nodded feebly. Evidently he was fast lapsing into unconsciousness again. But with an effort he managed to concentrate his mind upon what the Countess was saying.

===Chinese===
西尔瓦虚弱地点了点头。显然，他正在迅速再度陷入昏迷。但他强打精神，竭力把心思集中到伯爵夫人所说的话上。

===Original===
“The diary is locked up in a little desk in your bedroom,” the Countess went on. “I am to find it and give it to Mr. Walter Lance to read. My good Silva, this is most extraordinary! What possible interest could Mr. Lance take in that diary? Are you quite sure that I have not made a mistake?”

===Chinese===
“那本日记锁在你卧室里的一只小书桌里，”伯爵夫人接着说，“要我找到它，交给沃尔特·兰斯（Walter Lance）先生过目。我的好西尔瓦，这可太不寻常了！兰斯先生对那本日记能有什么相干？你敢肯定我没有弄错？”

===Original===
Again Silva opened his eyes and nodded almost vigorously.

===Chinese===
西尔瓦又睁开眼睛，点头点得几乎称得上用力。

===Original===
“Very well,” the Countess said reluctantly. “I see you are in earnest. I will get the diary at once, and Mr. Lance shall have it without delay. If there is anything more﻿—”

===Chinese===
“好吧，”伯爵夫人不情愿地说，“我看你是认真的。我这就去取日记，立刻交到兰斯先生手上。若是还有别的吩咐——”

===Original===
It was idle to speak to Silva any longer. Just for an instant a smile flickered over his face, and then he was completely lost to the world and his surroundings. Puzzled and mystified, the Countess crept from the room. Silva had made this request on what was practically his dying bed, and he must be obeyed. What good it would do at this moment the Countess was quite at a loss to see. She found the little desk presently and broke it open. Inside lay a small parchment-covered volume with gilt lettering on the outside. With this in her hand the Countess Flavio walked out on to the lawn where Walter was strolling up and down and accosted him.

===Chinese===
再对西尔瓦说什么也是徒劳了。刹那间，一抹微笑闪过他的脸，随后他便彻底失去了对尘世与周遭的一切知觉。伯爵夫人满腹狐疑、困惑不解，蹑手蹑脚地退出了房间。西尔瓦这几乎是在临终的病榻上提出的请求，不能不照办。至于眼下这样做有什么好处，伯爵夫人全然捉摸不透。她很快找到了那只小书桌，把它撬开。里面放着一本羊皮封面的小册子，外皮上带着烫金的字。弗拉维奥伯爵夫人把它拿在手里，走到草坪上沃尔特正来回踱步的地方，开口同他搭话。

===Original===
“This is for you,” she said. “I don’t know why, but Silva told me to deliver it into your hands, and perhaps when you have read it you will have a different opinion of Vera’s mother.”

===Chinese===
“这个给你，”她说，“我不知道为什么，西尔瓦吩咐我把它交到你的手上；也许等你读过之后，你对薇拉的母亲会另有一番看法。”

## XII A Speaking Likeness / 第十二章 会说话的画像

===Original===
It was some little time before Lord Ravenspur replied. For a moment or two he seemed to be bereft of the gift of speech.

===Chinese===
拉文斯珀勋爵隔了好一会儿才开口回答。有那么一两刻，他仿佛连说话的本领都丧失了。

===Original===
“It sounds almost incredible,” he managed to stammer at length. “You are absolutely certain you are not mistaken?”

===Chinese===
"这听起来简直叫人难以置信，"他终于结结巴巴地挤出话来。"你敢绝对肯定，自己没有弄错？"

===Original===
“No, I am not mistaken. Mrs. Delahay’s face is far too striking a one to be taken for that of anybody else. Of course, I am not asking you to give me any information. I am not seeking to pry into your secrets; but this mystery maddens me. The most extraordinary part of the whole affair is this﻿—for three years on and off I have known Mrs. Delahay intimately. I saw a great deal of her in Florence, also in Paris last year. And she has always given me the impression of being absolutely straightforward and single minded. And now, for some reason or another, she has taken it into her head to tell deliberate lies which appear to have no point or meaning. If she had only said that she went to call upon a friend after her husband had gone out, no further question would have been asked. Of course, I had not forgotten the evidence of the man Stevens. I must confess I should like to see him and ask him a few pointed questions. But apart from all that, you must see the necessity of getting Mrs. Delahay to tell the truth. It is just possible that she is shielding somebody. It is just possible that the whole thing is capable of explanation. But of that you are the best judge.”

===Chinese===
"不，我没有弄错。德拉海夫人的那张脸太惹眼了，绝不会被错认成别人的脸。当然，我并不是要你向我提供任何消息。我并不想刺探你的秘密；可这团迷雾简直要把我逼疯了。整桩事情里最不可思议的一点是——三年来断断续续，我一直同德拉海夫人交往密切。在佛罗伦萨（Florence）我时常与她相见，去年在巴黎也是如此。她一向给我的印象是：为人绝对直率坦诚，毫无心机。可如今，不知出于什么缘故，她却执意编起蓄意的谎话来，而且这些谎话看上去毫无目的、毫无意义。她当初只要说一句，丈夫出门之后她去拜访了一位朋友，那就再没有人会多问半句。当然，那个叫史蒂文斯（Stevens）的家伙所作的证词，我并没有忘记。我必须承认，我很想见见他，问他几个一针见血的问题。不过撇开这一切不谈，你必须看到，让德拉海夫人说出实话已是刻不容缓。说不定她是在包庇什么人；说不定整件事情都解释得通。但究竟如何，你最有发言权。"

===Original===
“It is a miserable business altogether,” Ravenspur groaned. “I am obliged to you for the straightforward way in which you have told me everything, and I will do my best with Maria Delahay. She refused to see me this morning, but I will go round after dinner and make another attempt to get an interview.”

===Chinese===
"这整桩事真是糟心透了，"拉文斯珀呻吟道。"你把一切原原本本、直截了当地告诉了我，我很感激；对玛丽亚·德拉海，我会尽力而为。今天上午她拒绝见我，但晚饭后我会再登门，另想办法见她一面。"

===Original===
It was somewhat later in the evening that Walter looked up his friend Venables again. As he expected, he found the journalist to be greatly interested in the Delahay case. Walter had debated the matter over in his mind. He could see no harm in telling Venables what he had discovered.

===Chinese===
当晚又过了些时候，沃尔特再次去寻访他的朋友维纳布斯。不出所料，他发现这位记者对德拉海一案大感兴趣。沃尔特已在心里把这件事反复掂量过，看不出把自己发现的情况告诉维纳布斯有什么害处。

===Original===
“It is certainly a curious case,” the latter remarked. “And professional interests apart, I should like to get to the bottom of this mystery. But I see you have some suggestion to make in connection with it. What is your idea?”

===Chinese===
"这案子确实离奇，"后者说道。"而且撇开职业上的兴趣不谈，我也想把这件疑案查个水落石出。不过我看得出来，你对此已有某种建议要提。你有什么想法？"

===Original===
“Well, I have been thinking it out as I came along,” Walter explained; “and it seems to me that we might get a good deal out of the witness John Stevens. He is the sort of man who would do anything for money, and a sovereign or two ought to loosen his tongue. I don’t want to say anything unkind about Louis Delahay, because he was a great friend of ours; and, so far as I know, his past is a clean and honourable one. But then you never can tell. What is a man like that doing to make an enemy, who is prepared to run the risk of being hanged for killing him? And why does he want to go round to his studio at such an hour in the morning?”

===Chinese===
"是这样，我一路走来时把这件事细细想了一遍，"沃尔特解释道，"依我看，我们可以从证人约翰·史蒂文斯身上掏出不少东西。他是那种为了钱什么都肯干的人，一两个金镑就该能撬开他的嘴。我不想说路易斯·德拉海（Louis Delahay）的坏话，因为他是我们的一位挚友；而且据我所知，他的过去清白而体面。不过话又说回来，这种事谁也说不准。像他那样的人，究竟做了什么事，才结下这样一个仇家——一个甘愿冒着上绞架的风险也要杀他的仇家？还有，他为什么要在清晨那么个时辰绕到自己的画室去？"

===Original===
“I thought of all that,” Venables said grimly. “Depend upon it, your unfortunate friend had some secret chapters in his life of which the world will probably never know anything. But what has all this got to do with that fellow Stevens?”

===Chinese===
"这些我都想到了，"维纳布斯冷冷地说。"你那位不幸的朋友，一生中准有些隐秘的篇章，世人恐怕永远无从知晓。可这一切跟史蒂文斯那个家伙又有什么相干？"

===Original===
“I was just coming to that point. If I had been the coroner I should have asked Stevens a great many more questions this morning. As it was, the authorities seemed content to let him go after he had given evidence to the effect that he had seen Mrs. Delahay with her husband. He told the court that he had been prowling and spying about Fitzjohn Square for some months, and he gave a pretty plain hint to the effect that he could tell a story or two about some of the inhabitants there. Now, for six months or more before Delahay went to Florence to be married, he lived a bachelor life at this house; and all this time Stevens was prowling about the neighbourhood after dark. It is not a very pleasant thing to have to do, but I should like to talk the matter over with Stevens and see if he can give us any information as regards Delahay. If you will telephone to Scotland Yard and get them to give you Stevens’ address, we will go round to his rooms and interview him at once.”

===Chinese===
"我正要说到这一点。今天上午若是换了我是验尸官，我还会多问史蒂文斯许许多多问题。而实际情况是，他作证说见过德拉海夫人同她丈夫在一起，当局似乎便心满意足，放他走了。他告诉法庭，几个月来他一直在菲茨约翰广场一带转悠窥探，还相当露骨地暗示：那一带某些居民的几段故事，他是能讲上一讲的。你要知道，德拉海去佛罗伦萨结婚之前，有六个月或者更长的时间，一直在这幢房子里过着单身生活；而这段时间里，史蒂文斯天黑之后总在附近一带游荡窥伺。这种事做起来不大痛快，但我愿意同史蒂文斯把这件事谈一谈，看看他能不能给我们提供一些关于德拉海的消息。你若肯打电话给苏格兰场，请他们把史蒂文斯的地址告诉你，我们就立刻到他寓所去见他。"

===Original===
It was no difficult matter to get the address in question, and presently the two friends reached the shabby house in the dingy street where Stevens lived. An exceedingly dirty child informed the visitors that Mr. Stevens was out at present, but that he always left his whereabouts behind him in case he might be required professionally. At the present moment, the precocious child informed the strangers, Mr. Stevens could be found at the Imperial Palace Theatre in Vauxhall Bridge Road.

===Chinese===
要到这个地址并不费事，不多一会儿，两位朋友便来到了那条晦暗的街道，走进史蒂文斯所住的破房子。一个脏得出奇的孩子告诉来客：史蒂文斯先生眼下外出了，不过他总会留下话说明自己的去向，以防有人因事务需要找他。这位少年老成的孩子又告诉两位陌生人，此时此刻，要找史蒂文斯先生，可以到沃克斯豪尔桥路（Vauxhall Bridge Road）的帝国宫剧院（Imperial Palace Theatre）去。
===Original===
“That is a bit of a coincidence,” Venables remarked. “However, we can’t do better than go down to the theatre.”

===Chinese===
"这倒有点巧，"维纳布斯说。"不过，我们不妨就到那家剧院去。"

===Original===
There was some little trouble in finding Stevens, and the performance was nearly at an end before he was pointed out to Walter by one of the attendants. He appeared to be none too sober, judging by his flushed face and somewhat unsteady gait; though, since the morning, his wardrobe had undergone a decided change for the better. The greasy, seedy frock-coat had vanished. Also the dilapidated silk hat. In fact the man looked quite prosperous.

===Chinese===
找史蒂文斯颇费了一番周折，直到演出将近尾声，才有一名侍者把他指给沃尔特看。单看他那张涨红的脸和略见踉跄的步态，这人显然已有几分醉意；不过，自早晨以来，他的行头倒是明显地上了一个台阶。那件油腻寒酸的长礼服不见了，那顶破旧的丝质大礼帽也不见了。事实上，这人看上去颇有一副阔气的派头。

===Original===
“I would suggest that we don’t speak to him in here,” Venables said. “Let us follow him out into the road.”

===Chinese===
"我建议我们别在这儿同他讲话，"维纳布斯说。"让我们跟着他到外面街上去。"

===Original===
Walter fell in at once with the idea. In the road Stevens paused as if waiting for somebody, and presently from the stage door there appeared the slim, graceful figure of Valdo. For some moments the two men stood in earnest conversation together, and from their attitude it was plainly evident that they were in hot dispute upon some point. The discussion lasted some little time. Then with a shrug of his shoulders, Valdo put his hand in his pocket and passed a coin or two over to his companion. Stevens was understood to say something to the effect that that would suffice for the present. Then he lounged off down the road and paused presently before a public-house which glittered invitingly opposite.

===Chinese===
沃尔特立刻赞同了这个主意。到了街上，史蒂文斯停住脚步，像是在等什么人；不多一会儿，后台门里出现了瓦尔多（Valdo）那瘦削优雅的身影。两人站着热切地交谈了片刻，单看他们那副架势便一清二楚：他们正为某个问题争执得不可开交。这场争论持续了一阵子。随后，瓦尔多耸了耸肩，把手伸进口袋，把一两枚硬币递给了同伴。史蒂文斯说了句什么，大意是眼下这些就够用了。然后他懒洋洋地沿街走去，不多时便在对面一家灯火诱人的酒馆前停了下来。

===Original===
“Catch him before he goes in there,” Venables whispered hurriedly. “If the fellow has any more to drink he will be perfectly useless to us for the rest of the evening.”

===Chinese===
"趁他还没进去，赶紧拦住他，"维纳布斯急忙低声道。"这家伙要是再多喝一口，今晚剩下的工夫对我们就算全完了。"

===Original===
Stevens turned suspiciously as Walter spoke to him.

===Chinese===
沃尔特向他开口时，史蒂文斯狐疑地转过身来。

===Original===
“I think your name is Stevens,” the latter said. “My friend here is a journalist and is greatly interested in the Fitzjohn Square mystery. We have been reading your evidence of this morning, and have come to the conclusion that you may be able to afford us some useful information. If you will answer a few questions we will make it worth your while.”

===Chinese===
"我想你叫史蒂文斯，"后者说道。"我这位朋友是记者，对菲茨约翰广场之谜极感兴趣。我们读了你今天上午的证词，得出的结论是：你也许能给我们提供一些有用的消息。你若肯回答几个问题，我们必不叫你白费功夫。"

===Original===
“To the extent of a couple of sovereigns,” Venables put in.

===Chinese===
"两个金镑的价码，"维纳布斯插话道。

===Original===
“Then I am your man,” Stevens exclaimed with alacrity. “Perhaps you wouldn’t mind coming round as far as my rooms. I have got a pretty poor memory for things, so I always jot everything down in my diary. I put everything down pretty well, because you never know what information is likely to be useful. I once made fifty pounds out of the simple fact that I saw a footman reading some postcards he was posting. Since then I have neglected no trifles.”

===Chinese===
"那我就是你们要的人，"史蒂文斯爽快地叫道。"也许二位不介意绕到我的寓所去一趟。我的记性差得很，所以总是把什么都记在日记里。我差不多样样都记，因为你永远说不准哪条消息会派上用场。有一回，就凭我看见一个男仆偷读他要寄出的明信片这么件小事，我就挣了五十镑。打那以后，任何细枝末节我都不肯放过了。"

===Original===
“What we want,” Walter explained, “is all you can tell us about Mr. Louis Delahay. You know him very well by sight, and you must be acquainted with some of his habits.”

===Chinese===
"我们要的，"沃尔特解释道，"是你所能讲出的关于路易斯·德拉海先生的一切。你同他见面熟得很，他的某些习惯，你也必定知晓一二。"
===Original===
Stevens laughed knowingly, and nodded his head.

===Chinese===
史蒂文斯会心一笑，点了点头。

===Original===
“I could open your eyes about a few of them in that neighbourhood,” he said. “I haven’t been loafing about Fitzjohn Square all these months for nothing. If I were a blackmailer, which I am not, I could live on the fat of the land. That is too dangerous a game to play, and I prefer to get along as I am.”

===Chinese===
"那一带居民的几桩事，我随便就能让二位开开眼，"他说。"这好几个月我在菲茨约翰广场闲逛，可不是白逛的。我要是个敲诈勒索的——我可不是——我早就可以过锦衣玉食的日子了。那种把戏玩起来太危险，我还是宁愿像现在这样混下去。"

===Original===
The man was evidently in a condition when he was past concealing anything. He chattered away glibly until his rooms were reached. Then with a flourish he opened the door and invited his visitors to enter. He apologised for the fact that he had nothing whereon to entertain the strangers, which apology was duly accepted. It was, perhaps, on the whole, a fortunate thing that Stevens’ cellar was empty. He ushered his companions into a grimy room, stuffy from want of air, and reeking with the odour of stale tobacco smoke.

===Chinese===
这人显然已经到了无所掩饰的地步。他一路伶牙俐齿地闲扯个不停，直到寓所门口。随后他神气十足地开了门，请两位来客进屋。他道歉说，自己没有任何东西可以用来款待客人；这份歉意也照单被收下了。总的说来，史蒂文斯的酒窖空空如也，兴许倒是一件幸事。他把两位同伴领进一间污秽的屋子，屋里因不通风而闷浊不堪，弥漫着隔夜烟草的臭味。

===Original===
“You will excuse me for a moment,” he said politely. “I will go into my bedroom and get my diary. I suppose pretty well all you want to know has happened quite lately.”

===Chinese===
"请恕我失陪片刻，"他客气地说。"我去卧室把日记本取来。我想，你们想知道的事，差不多都是最近才发生的。"

===Original===
“It is the last six months with which we are chiefly concerned,” Walter explained. “Before that does not matter.”

===Chinese===
"我们主要关心的正是最近这六个月，"沃尔特解释道。"再往前的就不打紧了。"

===Original===
Stevens turned away and closed the door behind him. He was gone some little time, so that his visitors had ample opportunity to take stock of their surroundings.
